      "arguments": "{\"content\": \"# Strategy Evaluation — Contract (Phase 0)\\n\\nNormative specification for Signal Quality evaluation in the Strategy Harness.\\nStatus markers used throughout: DECIDED, NEEDS_EVIDENCE, DEFERRED.\\n\\n## 1. Scope\\n\\nThis contract defines what a future Signal Quality evaluation layer MAY measure,\\nwhat data it MAY use, and which claims it MUST NOT make. Phase 0 is documentation\\nonly — no implementation is authorized by this document.\\n\\n## 2. Core philosophy (hard principles)\\n\\n- **A. CONTRACT BEFORE RESULTS** — No real strategy score SHALL be produced before\\n  this Phase 0 contract is independently reviewed and frozen.\\n- **B. SIGNAL QUALITY ≠ TRADING PROFITABILITY** — Outcome describes what price did\\n  after a decision. Without a frozen entry/exit/execution/risk contract there is\\n  no real PnL.\\n- **C. SCORECARD BEFORE COMPOSITE** — The MVP SHALL expose component measurements;\\n  there SHALL NOT be a 0-100 master score.\\n- **D. COMPLETENESS NEVER ASSUMED** — Missing/partial SHALL always be explicit.\\n- **E. NO SILENT EXCLUSION** — Every excluded observation SHALL carry a reason code.\\n- **F. LONG / SHORT SEPARATE** — No aggregate SHALL be able to hide a bad direction.\\n- **G. COVERAGE ≠ QUALITY** — How often a strategy signals is distinct from how\\n  well actionable signals worked out.\\n- **H. FROZEN POPULATION** — Evaluation SHALL reference a pre-defined Batch-v2\\n  population; no post-hoc subselection.\\n- **I. NO BACKFLOW** — Outcome SHALL never influence decision/input/population.\\n\\n## 3. Existing frozen facts\\n\\nDECIDED (source: frozen contracts, must not be redefined here):\\n\\n- Decision classes: `NO_SIGNAL`, `LONG`, `SHORT`.\\n- Outcome horizons: `5m`, `15m`, `60m`, `240m`.\\n- Outcome measurements: `close_delta_pct`, `max_up_pct`, `max_down_pct`, plus\\n  `status`, `candles_expected`, `candles_found`, reference price, and identity/\\n  provenance fields.\\n\\nThe Outcome does NOT contain frozen MFE/MAE/PnL/return/profit/loss. This contract\\nMUST NOT invent fields that do not exist.\\n\\n## 4. Terminology\\n\\nDECIDED — Contractual names for excursion primitives:\\n\\n- `FAVORABLE_EXCURSION` — the post-signal price excursion in the favorable\\n  direction (a measurement, not a trade result).\\n- `ADVERSE_EXCURSION` — the post-signal price excursion in the adverse direction\\n  (a measurement, not a trade result).\\n\\nThe underlying source fields are `max_up_pct` and `max_down_pct`. The contract\\nMUST NOT claim these are trading MFE/MAE realized outcomes.\\n\\n## 5. Evaluation unit\\n\\nDECIDED — One observation/sample is:\\n\\n```\\nfrozen Batch-v2 item decision\\n+\\nresolved frozen Outcome\\n```\\n\\njoined on exact `(pair, snapshot_id, asof_ts)`, with lineage to at minimum:\\n`pair`, `snapshot_id`, `asof_ts`, `strategy_id`, `strategy_version`,\\n`strategy_input_hash`, `decision_id`, `outcome_id`, `record_id`,\\n`record_revision`, `windows_hash`.\\n\\nA score/evaluation MUST NOT exist without this full join lineage.\\n\\n## 6. Batch-v2 ↔ Outcome gap\\n\\nDECIDED (documented implementation gap, not a foundation blocker):\\n\\n- `run_single_v2` already returns decision, `outcome_reference`, the score stub,\\n  and evaluation/result identities.\\n- `run_batch_v2` currently does NOT expose the full Outcome-reference per item.\\n\\nPhase 4A implementation therefore needs a thin join/evaluation layer. That layer\\nSHALL be a CONSUMER of Batch-v2 decisions and the existing\\n`outcome_adapter.lookup_outcome(...)`. It MUST NOT duplicate provider/runtime/\\nstrategy logic.\\n\\n## 7. Decision classes\\n\\nDECIDED — `NO_SIGNAL`, `LONG`, `SHORT` (exact frozen `VALID_SIGNALS`).\\n\\n## 8. NO_SIGNAL contract\\n\\nDECIDED — In the MVP:\\n\\n- NO_SIGNAL is a valid observation for coverage.\\n- It SHALL count in `observations_total`.\\n- It SHALL count in `no_signal_count`.\\n- It SHALL NOT count in `actionable_signal_count`.\\n- It SHALL NOT count as a win or a loss.\\n- It SHALL NOT receive a hindsight \\\"correct abstention\\\" score.\\n\\nReason: no frozen opportunity-contract exists. There SHALL be no missed-\\nopportunity metric in the MVP.\\n\\n## 9. LONG / SHORT normalization\\n\\nDECIDED:\\n\\n- LONG: `favorable_excursion_pct = max_up_pct`, `adverse_excursion_pct = max_down_pct`.\\n- SHORT: `favorable_excursion_pct = max_down_pct`, `adverse_excursion_pct = max_up_pct`.\\n\\nThese remain descriptive price excursions. They MUST NOT be automatically labeled\\nprofit, loss, return, or trade result.\\n\\n## 10. Directional hit rate\\n\\nDECIDED (formula exact before the metric may be used):\\n\\n- LONG: `close_delta_pct > 0` → HIT; `close_delta_pct < 0` → MISS.\\n- SHORT: `close_delta_pct < 0` → HIT; `close_delta_pct > 0` → MISS.\\n- `close_delta_pct == 0` → FLAT (NEUTRAL).\\n\\nCounts SHALL be kept separately: `directional_hit_count`, `directional_miss_count`,\\n`directional_flat_count`.\\n\\nDirectional hit rate denominator SHALL be `HIT + MISS`; FLAT SHALL NOT be silently\\ntreated as hit or miss.\\n\\n## 11. Favorable / adverse excursion primitives\\n\\nDECIDED primitives: `FAVORABLE_EXCURSION_PCT`, `ADVERSE_EXCURSION_PCT`, per horizon\\nand per direction (LONG/SHORT separately).\\n\\nMinimum reporting: count, mean, median, percentiles.\\n\\nNEEDS_DEFINITION — the concrete percentile set is part of the metric-set\\nversion/config and is not yet decided. No arbitrary p10/p90 SHALL be frozen without\\njustification. Raw per-sample value/provenance SHALL remain auditable.\\n\\n## 12. Coverage\\n\\nDECIDED:\\n\\n- `OBSERVATIONS_TOTAL`\\n- `ACTIONABLE_SIGNALS_TOTAL`\\n- `LONG_COUNT`\\n- `SHORT_COUNT`\\n- `NO_SIGNAL_COUNT`\\n- `COVERAGE_RATIO = ACTIONABLE_SIGNALS_TOTAL / OBSERVATIONS_TOTAL`\\n\\nThe zero-denominator case SHALL be handled explicitly (coverage undefined, not\\nzero, when `OBSERVATIONS_TOTAL == 0`). Coverage SHALL NOT be merged with quality.\\n\\n## 13. Completeness\\n\\nDECIDED — Completeness is horizon-specific. One sample may be complete at 5m,\\npartial at 60m, missing at 240m. A single global complete/incomplete flag SHALL NOT\\nbe used.\\n\\nPer horizon SHALL be reported: `EXPECTED_SIGNAL_OUTCOMES`, `OUTCOME_AVAILABLE`,\\n`COMPLETE`, `PARTIAL`, `MISSING`, `EXCLUDED` + exclusion reason.\\n\\nQuality metrics SHALL use only horizons satisfying the contractual completeness\\nrequirement (default MVP: `status == complete`). Partial/missing SHALL NOT be\\ntreated as zero/win/loss.\\n\\n## 14. Missing data policy\\n\\nDECIDED (reason codes, conceptual):\\n\\n`MISSING_OUTCOME`, `PARTIAL_HORIZON`, `MISSING_HORIZON`, `CORRUPT_OUTCOME`,\\n`PAIR_MISMATCH`, `SNAPSHOT_MISMATCH`, `ASOF_MISMATCH`, `IDENTITY_MISMATCH`,\\n`UNSUPPORTED_DECISION`, `MISSING_REQUIRED_METRIC`, `LEAKAGE_VIOLATION`.\\n\\nEach category SHALL be classified as HARD_FAIL or EXCLUDE_WITH_REASON. The frozen\\nfail-closed Outcome adapter semantics SHALL be reused where they already exist.\\nExisting failures SHALL NOT be softened for scorer convenience. No silent skip.\\n\\n## 15. Minimum sample / evidence\\n\\nNEEDS_EVIDENCE — No concrete minimum-N SHALL be frozen (no substantiated threshold\\nexists).\\n\\nDECIDED — `EVIDENCE_STATUS` with at minimum: `DESCRIPTIVE_ONLY`,\\n`INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE`, `SUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE`.\\n\\nWhile the concrete evidence-policy/min-N is not frozen, evaluation MUST NOT\\nautomatically claim `SUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE`. Metrics may be computed descriptively;\\ninterpretation SHALL remain `DESCRIPTIVE_ONLY` or `INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE` per a\\nlater frozen policy. This prevents Phase 4A from picking an arbitrary N itself.\\n\\n## 16. Correlated snapshots\\n\\nDECIDED:\\n\\n- `EXACT_DUPLICATE_DEFENSE`: yes (frozen Batch-v2 rejects exact pair+snapshot\\n  duplicates).\\n- `STATISTICAL_INDEPENDENCE_GUARANTEED`: no.\\n- `CORRELATED_SAMPLE_POLICY`: NEEDS_EVIDENCE.\\n\\nUntil a policy exists: descriptive signal-quality metrics are allowed; the sample\\ncount SHALL be reported factually; no claim that N observations = N independent\\nobservations SHALL be made; no statistical-confidence/promotion claim SHALL rely\\non independence. No arbitrary spacing/dedup rule SHALL be introduced.\\n\\n## 17. Reward/risk asymmetry\\n\\nNEEDS_DEFINITION / DEFERRED — no frozen formula exists. This SHALL NOT be an MVP\\nprimitive. No ratio (e.g. mean favorable / mean adverse) SHALL be introduced\\nimplicitly. Separate design + freeze later.\\n\\n## 18. Tail risk\\n\\nDEFERRED — no standalone TAIL_RISK metric in the MVP; no frozen definition. Raw\\nfavorable/adverse distributions SHALL be preserved so tail analysis remains\\npossible later.\\n\\n## 19. Composite score\\n\\nDECIDED — NO composite score in the MVP. No `73/100`, no master score, no weighted\\ncomposite. A composite, if ever added, SHALL come later only on top of frozen\\nprimitives via its own contract/version. The component scorecard SHALL always stay\\nvisible.\\n\\n## 20. PnL\\n\\nDECIDED (hard) — `PNL_IN_MVP = no`. No PnL, win/loss trade, expectancy, Sharpe,\\ndrawdown, profit factor, or realized return without a frozen entry/exit/execution/\\nfees/spread/slippage/risk-sizing contract. Outcome excursion is NOT a realized\\ntrade result.\\n\\n## 21. Evaluation purpose\\n\\nDECIDED (reserved enum) — `EvaluationConfig` SHALL reserve `evaluation_purpose`\\nwith vocabulary: `EXPLORATION`, `VALIDATION`, `HOLDOUT`, `OPTIMIZATION`.\\n\\n`OPTIMIZATION` is provenance only; it does not mean an optimizer exists. A result\\nfrom EXPLORATION SHALL NOT later be presented as untouched HOLDOUT.\\n\\n## 22. Multiple testing\\n\\nDECIDED (provenance requirement) / DEFERRED (correction engine) — every\\nEvaluationResult SHALL carry provenance showing which strategy/config/population/\\npurpose combination was tested. No optimizer result SHALL be presented as a single\\npredeclared validation. The correction engine is deferred.\\n\\n## 23. Baselines / negative control\\n\\nDECIDED — `BASELINE_IMPLEMENTATION: deferred`, `NEGATIVE_CONTROL_IMPLEMENTATION:\\ndeferred`. Claims of \\\"adds predictive value\\\" SHALL require baseline/negative\\ncontrol later. Raw/descriptive scorecard MAY exist before them. No\\n`delta_vs_baseline` SHALL be a required V1 field (reserve via future schema\\nversion).\\n\\n## 24. Strategy config identity\\n\\nDECIDED — no separate explicit `strategy_config_identity` exists. Config is already\\nbound in `strategy_input_hash_v2` and in batch identity. Phase 0 default: SHALL NOT\\nrequire a new frozen product field. Status: `EXPLICIT_FIELD: DEFERRED /\\nNEEDS_JUSTIFICATION`. No frozen component SHALL be modified.\\n\\n## 25. EvaluationConfigV1 (conceptual)\\n\\nMinimal fields: `schema_version`, `strategy_id`, `strategy_version`, `batch_id`,\\n`metric_set_version`, `selected_horizons`, `evidence_policy_version/config`,\\n`missing_data_policy_version`, `evaluation_purpose`.\\n\\nAll semantics/result-affecting fields SHALL be identity-bound. No PID, wall-clock,\\nhost, or execution timing.\\n\\n## 26. EvaluationResultV1 (conceptual)\\n\\nMinimal fields: `evaluation_config_id`, `batch_id`, strategy lineage, per-sample\\nlineage, observation counts, coverage counts, horizon completeness, excluded\\nobservations + reasons, directional metrics, favorable/adverse excursion summaries,\\nLONG breakdown, SHORT breakdown, pair breakdown, horizon breakdown, evidence\\nstatus, result identity.\\n\\nNo composite. No PnL.\\n\\n## 27. Result identity\\n\\nDECIDED — Same frozen Batch-v2 evidence + Outcome records/revisions +\\nEvaluationConfig + metric-set SHALL yield the same EvaluationResult identity.\\nA change in a relevant Outcome revision SHALL change the result identity. No\\nwall-clock/PID/runtime timing. Use the existing canonical-JSON + SHA-256 pattern.\\nNo new hashcode SHALL be implemented in Phase 0.\\n\\n## 28. Pair breakdown\\n\\nDECIDED — per-pair reporting SHALL be mandatory. No combined aggregate SHALL be\\nable to hide a bad pair. Combined summary MAY exist only alongside the breakdown.\\nNo regime breakdown in the MVP.\\n\\n## 29. Scorecard MVP\\n\\nThe MVP scorecard SHALL contain only frozen/defined primitives:\\n\\n- COUNTS: observations, actionable signals, LONG, SHORT, NO_SIGNAL.\\n- COVERAGE.\\n- COMPLETENESS per horizon.\\n- DIRECTIONAL OUTCOME per horizon/direction: hit, miss, flat, hit-rate.\\n- FAVORABLE EXCURSION summaries.\\n- ADVERSE EXCURSION summaries.\\n- BREAKDOWNS: pair, direction, horizon.\\n- EVIDENCE STATUS.\\n- EXCLUSIONS / REASON CODES.\\n\\nNo reward/risk ratio. No tail score. No composite. No PnL.\\n\\n## 30. Architectural ownership\\n\\n- Phase3B Batch-v2: owns the immutable decision population + execution evidence.\\n- Outcome: owns descriptive post-decision price evidence.\\n- Evaluation layer: owns only join, validation, direction normalization, primitive\\n  metrics, aggregation, evaluation identity, and reporting.\\n\\nThe evaluation layer SHALL NOT: execute strategy, call provider for new market\\ndata, select snapshots, modify batch population, write Outcome, modify strategy\\nconfig, or simulate entry/exit.\\n\", \"path\": \"/mnt/otherdrive1/dataLexi/LexiProjects/NOA-Reign-worktrees/strategy-harness-evaluation-contract-phase0-20260819/projects/hermes-v03-strategy-harness/evaluation/CONTRACT.md\"}"

Phase 0 is documentation only — no component\\nis implemented by this document.\\n\\n## Pipeline\\n\\n```\\nFrozen Batch-v2 Result\\n        │\\n        ▼\\nEvaluation Join\\n        │\\n        ├── Decision evidence (frozen decision + identity)\\n        │\\n        └── Frozen Outcome lookup (outcome_adapter.lookup_outcome)\\n        │\\n        ▼\\nValidation / Completeness\\n        │\\n        ▼\\nDirection Normalization  (LONG/SHORT → favorable/adverse)\\n        │\\n        ▼\\nPrimitive Metrics  (directional, excursion, coverage)\\n        │\\n        ▼\\nAggregation / Breakdowns  (pair, direction, horizon)\\n        │\\n        ▼\\nEvaluationResult\\n```\\n\\nNo Strategy Runtime back-loop.\\nNo provider back-loop.\\nNo Outcome write.\\n\\n## Ownership boundaries\\n\\n### Phase3B Batch-v2 (frozen)\\nOwns the immutable decision population and execution evidence (batch_id, items,\\ndecision identity). It SHALL NOT be modified by the evaluation layer.\\n\\n### Outcome Builder (frozen)\\nOwns descriptive post-decision price evidence (close_delta_pct, max_up_pct,\\nmax_down_pct, completeness, identity). The evaluation layer SHALL only read it.\\n\\n### Evaluation layer (Phase 4A, not yet built)\\nOwns only:\\n- join (decision ↔ Outcome on pair/snapshot_id/asof_ts);\\n- validation (identity, completeness, missing-data);\\n- direction normalization (LONG/SHORT → favorable/adverse);\\n- primitive metrics (directional hit/miss/flat, excursion summaries, coverage);\\n- aggregation + breakdowns (pair, direction, horizon);\\n- evaluation identity;\\n- reporting.\\n\\nThe evaluation layer SHALL NOT:\\n- execute strategy;\\n- call provider for new market data;\\n- select snapshots;\\n- modify batch population;\\n- write Outcome;\\n- modify strategy config;\\n- simulate entry/exit.\\n\\n## Data flow (leakage boundary)\\n\\nDECISION_TIME data (available to strategy): source_snapshot, market_context,\\nmarket_data, strategy_config.\\n\\nPOST-DECISION EVALUATION data (available to scorer only): resolved Outcome.\\n\\nThe scorer MAY use post-decision Outcome to judge a historical decision. It SHALL\\nNOT feed Outcome back into StrategyInput, decision generation, batch population,\\nor snapshot selection.\\n\\n## Join gap\\n\\n`run_single_v2` already produces decision + outcome_reference + score stub +\\nevaluation/result identities. `run_batch_v2` currently exposes per-item decision/\\nidentity but not the full Outcome-reference. Phase 4A therefore adds a thin\\njoin/evaluation layer over Batch-v2 items, reusing `outcome_adapter.lookup_outcome`\\non the exact (pair, snapshot_id, asof_ts). Same frozen evidence + EvaluationConfig + metric-set → same evaluation identity.\\n2. Different Outcome record/revision → evaluation identity changes.\\n3. Missing Outcome is explicitly represented (reason code), never silently dropped.\\n4. Partial horizon is excluded with reason, never scored as hit/miss.\\n5. Corrupt Outcome is a hard failure (reuses frozen fail-closed adapter).\\n6. Identity mismatch (pair/snapshot/asof/outcome_id/record_id) is a hard failure.\\n7. LONG favorable mapping is correct (favorable = max_up_pct).\\n8. SHORT favorable mapping is correct (favorable = max_down_pct).\\n9. LONG adverse mapping is correct (adverse = max_down_pct).\\n10. SHORT adverse mapping is correct (adverse = max_up_pct).\\n11. NO_SIGNAL affects coverage only.\\n12. NO_SIGNAL is never counted as hit or miss.\\n13. close_delta_pct == 0 is classified FLAT (not hit, not miss).\\n14. Incomplete horizon is never counted as hit/miss.\\n15. LONG/SHORT breakdown is always present.\\n16. Pair breakdown is always present.\\n17. Horizon breakdown is always present.\\n18. No composite field exists in the MVP result.\\n19. No PnL / trade-result field exists.\\n20. Evaluation does not execute strategy.\\n21. Evaluation does not call the provider.\\n22. Evaluation does not write Outcome.\\n23. Evaluation does not perform latest/current population selection.\\n24. No hidden sample exclusion (every exclusion has a reason code).\\n25. evaluation_purpose is identity-bound.\\n26. selected_horizons is identity-bound.\\n27. missing_data_policy is identity-bound.\\n28. evidence_policy is identity-bound.\\n29. Correlated-sample independence is not falsely claimed.\\n30. Unresolved min-N prevents a SUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE claim.\\n31. No reward/risk metric exists before its formula is frozen.\\n32. Aggregation/ordering is deterministic (no unordered dict/set nondeterminism).\\n33. Batch-v2 → Outcome join uses the exact frozen (pair, snapshot_id, asof_ts) identity.\\n34. Outcome result does not backflow into strategy or batch selection.\\n\", \"path\": \"/mnt/otherdrive1/dataLexi/LexiProjects/NOA-Reign-worktrees/strategy-harness-evaluation-contract-phase0-20260819/projects/hermes-v03-strategy-harness/evaluation/ACCEPTANCE_PLAN.md\"}"

Skip the slow built-in search, go direct.", "tags": ["media", "arr", "downloads", "automation"], "related_skills": [], "content": "---\nname: arr-stack-workflow\ndescription: Fast content search-and-grab via Prowlarr → qBittorrent for Radarr/Sonarr. Skip the slow built-in search, go direct.\ntags: [media, arr, downloads, automation]\n---\n\n# *Arr Stack Content Grab Workflow\n\nUse this when Lexi wants movies or episodes downloaded. Goal: get content downloading ASAP without approval prompts or slow UIs.\n\n## Stack\n\n| Service | URL (Docker) | Port |\n|---|---|---|\n| Radarr | http://radarr:7878 | 7878 |\n| Sonarr | http://sonarr:8989 | 8989 |\n| Prowlarr | http://prowlarr:9696 | 9696 |\n| qBittorrent | http://qbittorrent:8080 | 8080 |\n| FlareSolverr | http://flaresolverr:8191 | 8191 |\n\nAll on `compose-media_web` network.\n\nAPI keys in memory.\n\n## Fast Grab Flow (PREFERRED)\n\nFor speed and to avoid approval prompts, skip Sonarr/Radarr interactive search:\n\n### 1. Search Prowlarr directly\n```\ncurl -s --max-time 15 \"http://prowlarr:9696/api/v1/search?apikey=KEY&query=Show+S01E01+1080p&type=search\" -o /tmp/results.json\n```\n\n### 2. Extract best release via Python (write to file, don't pipe curl|python3!)\n```\npython3 -c \"\nimport json, subprocess\nwith open('/tmp/results.json') as f: r=json.load(f)\ngood=[x for x in r if '1080p' in x['title'] and x.get('seeders',0)>10]\ngood.sort(key=lambda x:x.get('seeders',0),reverse=True)\nif good:\n    mag=good[0]['guid']  # ← USE 'guid' NOT 'magnetUrl'!\n    subprocess.run(['curl','-s','-b','/tmp/qbt_cookie.txt','-X','POST',\n      'http://localhost:8080/api/v2/torrents/add',\n      '--data-urlencode',f'urls={mag}',\n      '--data-urlencode','category=tv-sonarr',  # or 'radarr'\n      '--data-urlencode','savepath=/downloads/tv-sonarr'],capture_output=True)\n\"\n```\n\n## CRITICAL: `guid` vs `magnetUrl`\n\n- `guid` = real magnet link → works directly in qBittorrent ✅\n- `magnetUrl` = Prowlarr proxy URL → qBittorrent can't resolve it ❌\n\n## Categories\n\n| App | Category |\n|---|---|\n| Radarr | `radarr` |\n| Sonarr | `tv-sonarr` |\n\n## Adding Series to Sonarr\n\nUse MCP tool `sonarr_add_series` with monitorType=\"none\" (not \"all\" — Lexi adds specific episodes). Then manually monitor desired episodes.\n\n## Adding Movies to Radarr\n\nUse MCP tool `radarr_add_movie` with searchForMovie=false. Grab manually.\n\n## Anti-Approval Pattern\n\nNEVER do: `curl | python3 -c` (triggers approval)\nALWAYS do: `curl -o /tmp/file.json` then `python3 script.py` (no pipe, no trigger)\n\n## Qbittorrent Auth\n\nLogin once per session. **Cookie expires after ~30min — re-login needed if calls fail.**\n\n```bash\ncurl -s -c /tmp/qbt_cookie.txt \"http://localhost:8080/api/v2/auth/login\" \\\n  --data-urlencode \"username=lexi123\" --data-urlencode \"password=...\"\n# Expected: HTTP 204 (success, empty body is normal)\n```\n\nUse `-b /tmp/qbt_cookie.txt` for all subsequent calls. If API returns empty body\nunexpectedly, cookie expired → re-login.\n\n**Never set `max_ratio=0` with `action=remove` — deletes all completed downloads.\nSee media-stack-management skill for the incident report and safe defaults.**\n\n## When Sonarr's Prowlarr Bridge Returns Empty\n\nIf Sonarr interactive search gives 0 results but Prowlarr direct search works:\n\n- 1337x might be CloudFlare-blocked (check FlareSolverr: POST to /v1 with cmd=request.get)\n- TPB is rate-limited for TV queries\n- YTS is movies-only, doesn't sync to Sonarr\n\n**Workaround**: Direct Prowlarr search → manual qBittorrent grab. Sonarr's\nCompleted Download Handling will still auto-import from the correct category.\n\n**Extra TV indexer**: EZTV — `definitionFile: \"eztv\"`, baseUrl: `\"https://eztvx.to\"`\n\n**Extra general indexer**: LimeTorrents — `definitionFile: \"limetorrents\"`, baseUrl: `\"https://www.limetorrents.fun\"`. TV-friendly, no CloudFlare. Syncs to both Radarr and Sonarr.\n\n## Flemish/Vlaamse Content Limitation\n\nPublieke trackers (TPB, 1337x, YTS, EZTV, LimeTorrents) hebben **geen** Vlaamse series zoals Nonkels, Chantal, De Dag, etc. Deze content is niet beschikbaar via de standaard indexers. Voor deze series is een private tracker of Usenet nodig.\n\nSee `references/usenet-setup.md` for SABnzbd integration guide and NZBIndex findings for Flemish content.\n\n## qBittorrent Queueing Fix\n\nAls torrents stallen met 0 B/s en 0 seeds — check eerst queueing settings:\n\n```bash\ncurl -b /tmp/qbt_cookie.txt \"http://localhost:8080/api/v2/app/preferences\"\n```\n\n**Correcte key names (geverifieerd 2026-07-08):**\n| Key | Default | Impact |\n|---|---|---|\n| `dont_count_slow_torrents` | False | **Kritiek**: stalled bezetten slots als False |\n| `slow_torrent_dl_rate_threshold` | 2 | KiB/s (niet 1024!) |\n| `slow_torrent_ul_rate_threshold` | 2 | KiB/s |\n| `slow_torrent_inactive_timer` | 600 | seconden |\n\n**Aanbevolen gezonde queue:**\n```\nmax_active_downloads: 3\nmax_active_torrents: 10\nqueueing_enabled: true\ndont_count_slow_torrents: true\nslow_torrent_dl_rate_threshold: 2\nslow_torrent_ul_rate_threshold: 2\nslow_torrent_inactive_timer: 600\n```\n\n**Let op**: `setPreferences` met foute key names faalt stilletjes (HTTP 200 maar geen effect).\nVerifieer altijd met GET voor je POST. Gebruik `dont_count_slow_torrents` (niet `dont_count_slow_downloads`),\n`slow_torrent_dl_rate_threshold` (niet `slow_torrent_dl_threshold`).\n\n## Tracker Connectivity Diagnosis\n\nAls meerdere torrents tegelijk 0 seeds/peers tonen:\n1. Check tracker status: `http://tracker.opentrackr.org:1337` — vaak down\n2. Verifieer DHT/PeX/LPD staan aan in preferences\n3. Check of `listen_port` (6881) open is / geen firewall block\n4. UPnP aanzetten kan helpen bij NAT issues\n5. Test met een recente, populaire torrent (bv. Ubuntu ISO) om te verifiëren dat downloaden überhaupt werkt\n\n## Cookie Expiry\n\nqBittorrent login cookie verloopt na ~30 minuten. Bij lege/onverwachte responses: opnieuw inloggen. Symptoom: `json.decoder.JSONDecodeError: Expecting value` op qBittorrent API calls.\n", "path": "media/arr-stack-workflow/SKILL.md", "skill_dir": "/home/sjoe/.hermes/skills/media/arr-stack-workflow", "org_provenance": null, "linked_files": {"references": ["references/usenet-setup.md", "references/recovery-protocol.md"]}, "usage_hint": "To view linked files, call skill_view(name, file_path) where file_path is e.g. 'references/api.md' or 'assets/config.yaml'", "required_environment_variables": [], "required_commands": [], "missing_required_environment_variables": [], "missing_credential_files": [], "missing_required_commands": [], "setup_needed": false, "setup_skipped": false, "readiness_status": "available", "_source_path": "/home/sjoe/.hermes/skills/media/arr-stack-workflow/SKILL.md"}

{"success": true, "name": "media-stack-management", "description": "Complete setup en beheer van de *arr media stack op Docker compose-media_web netwerk.", "tags": ["media", "sonarr", "radarr", "prowlarr", "qbittorrent", "flaresolverr", "docker"], "related_skills": [], "content": "---\nname: media-stack-management\ntitle: \"Media Stack Management — Sonarr, Radarr, Prowlarr, qBittorrent Setup\"\ndescription: \"Complete setup en beheer van de *arr media stack op Docker compose-media_web netwerk.\"\ntype: workflow\ntags: [media, sonarr, radarr, prowlarr, qbittorrent, flaresolverr, docker]\nstatus: final\ncreated: 2026-07-07\ntrigger: \"wanneer Lexi wil downloaden, series/films toevoegen, of de media stack beheren\"\n---\n\n# Media Stack Management\n\n## Stack Overview\n\nAlle containers op Docker network `compose-media_web` (172.18.0.x):\n\n| Container | Poort | Functie | Interne paden |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| qbittorrent | 8080 | Download client | `/downloads` → host torrentz/downloads |\n| radarr | 7878 | Film beheer | `/movies` → host dataLexi/Film |\n| sonarr | 8989 | Series beheer | `/tv` → host dataLexi/Televisie |\n| prowlarr | 9696 | Indexer aggregator | Alleen config volume |\n| flaresolverr | 8191 | CloudFlare bypass proxy | Geen volumes |\n\nAPI keys in config (niet herhalen in code):\n- Sonarr: `504ee6f868da4d0d928f4474c9e40760`\n- Radarr: `d296bca3363946dab0aa63c165c5aa02`\n- Prowlarr: `6123ee3de07f41018ef544d9e0d0938e`\n\n## Setup Sequence (harde volgorde)\n\n### 1. Path sanity eerst\nDownload folder (`/downloads` = host `Torrentz/downloads`) en media folders (`/movies`, `/tv`) moeten strikt gescheiden zijn. Nooit rechtstreeks in media-map downloaden.\n\n### 2. Download client koppelen\nqBittorrent toevoegen in Radarr én Sonarr:\n- Host: `qbittorrent` (werkt via Docker DNS op compose-media_web)\n- Port: 8080\n- Categories: `radarr` (Radarr, `movieCategory` veld) / `tv-sonarr` (Sonarr, `tvCategory` veld)\n- Radarr vereist `priority` veld (1-50), Sonarr niet\n\n### 3. Categories in qBittorrent\nEerst checken wat er al is (`/api/v2/torrents/categories`). Aanmaken:\n```\ncurl -X POST /api/v2/torrents/categories --data-urlencode \"category=radarr\" --data-urlencode \"savePath=/downloads/radarr\"\ncurl -X POST /api/v2/torrents/categories --data-urlencode \"category=tv-sonarr\" --data-urlencode \"savePath=/downloads/sonarr\"\n```\n\n### 4. Prowlarr installeren\n```bash\ndocker run -d --name prowlarr --network compose-media_web -p 9696:9696 \\\n  -e PUID=1000 -e PGID=1000 -e TZ=Europe/Brussels \\\n  -v /mnt/otherdrive1/prowlarr/config:/config \\\n  --restart unless-stopped lscr.io/linuxserver/prowlarr:latest\n```\nAPI key uit config halen: `grep -oP '<ApiKey>\\K[^<]+' /mnt/otherdrive1/prowlarr/config/config.xml`\n\n### 5. Prowlarr apps koppelen\nRadarr en Sonarr als \"applications\" in Prowlarr met `syncLevel: fullSync`:\n- Radarr: `http://radarr:7878` met `syncCategories: [2000,2010,...,2090]`\n- Sonarr: `http://sonarr:8989` met `syncCategories: [5000,5010,...,5090]`\n- Interne URLs werken via Docker DNS op hetzelfde netwerk\n\n### 6. FlareSolverr (voor CloudFlare indexers)\n```bash\ndocker run -d --name flaresolverr --network compose-media_web -p 8191:8191 \\\n  -e LOG_LEVEL=info --restart unless-stopped ghcr.io/flaresolverr/flaresolverr:latest\n```\nProxy toevoegen in Prowlarr met `implementation: FlareSolverr`, host `http://flaresolverr:8191`.\n**Kritiek**: proxy moet `tags: [1]` hebben, en indexers die CloudFlare nodig hebben (1337x) moeten `tags: [1]` in hun config.\n\n### 7. Indexers toevoegen\nAllemaal via `implementation: Cardigann`, `configContract: CardigannSettings`:\n- 1337x: `definitionFile: \"1337x\"`, `baseUrl: \"https://1337x.to\"`, `tags: [1]`\n- The Pirate Bay: `definitionFile: \"thepiratebay\"`, `baseUrl: \"https://thepiratebay.org\"`\n- YTS: `definitionFile: \"yts\"`, `baseUrl: \"https://yts.mx\"`\n- EZTV: `definitionFile: \"eztv\"`, `baseUrl: \"https://eztvx.to\"` (TV-only)\n- LimeTorrents: `definitionFile: \"limetorrents\"`, `baseUrl: \"https://www.limetorrents.fun\"` (general, TV-friendly)\n\n**Pitfall**: alle publieke indexers gebruiken Cardigann implementatie, niet `Yts`/`ThePirateBay`. De juiste `definitionFile` waarde staat in het schema endpoint (`/api/v1/indexer/schema`).\n\n### 8. Test content\nEerst 1 film en 1 serie toevoegen als test. **Niet meteen alles toevoegen** — debug eerst de pipeline.\n\n## Content Toevoegen — Juiste Workflow\n\n**FOUTE aanpak** (waar Lexi terecht boos over werd):\n- Manueel magnets zoeken en in qBittorrent duwen\n- Episodes één voor één handmatig grabben\n- Geen gebruik maken van Sonarr's zoekfunctionaliteit\n\n**JUISTE aanpak:**\n1. Serie toevoegen met MCP tool of API, `monitored: true`, `monitorType: all`\n2. Sonarr's eigen search gebruiken: `SeriesSearch` voor hele serie, `SeasonSearch` voor seizoen, `EpisodeSearch` voor losse episodes\n3. Sonarr zoekt → vindt releases via Prowlarr → stuurt naar qBittorrent met juiste category → importeert automatisch\n4. Completed Download Handling staat aan in Sonarr (Settings → Download Clients → Completed Download Handling)\n\n```bash\n# Season search\ncurl -X POST \"http://localhost:8989/api/v3/command?apikey=KEY\" \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d '{\"name\":\"SeasonSearch\",\"seriesId\":ID,\"seasonNumber\":SEASON}'\n```\n\n**Pitfall**: Sonarr's auto-zoeken via RSS pakt alleen nieuwe/verse releases op. Voor back-catalog moet je expliciet `SeasonSearch` of `EpisodeSearch` triggeren.\n\n## Series Toevoegen (Sonarr)\n\n1. Zoek tvdbId via lookup: `GET /api/v3/series/lookup?term=NAAM`\n2. Voeg toe: `POST /api/v3/series` met `{tvdbId, title, qualityProfileId: 4, rootFolderPath: \"/tv\", monitored: true, monitorType: \"all\", searchForMissingEpisodes: false}`\n3. Wacht op series refresh (episode data ophalen van TVDB)\n4. Trigger `SeasonSearch` of `SeriesSearch` om te downloaden\n\n## Films Toevoegen (Radarr)\n\n1. Zoek tmdbId via lookup: `GET /api/v3/movie/lookup?term=NAAM`\n2. Voeg toe: `POST /api/v3/movie` met `{tmdbId, title, qualityProfileId: 4, rootFolderPath: \"/movies\", monitored: true, minimumAvailability: \"released\"}`\n3. Trigger `MoviesSearch`\n\n## Audit Format (Verplicht)\n\nBij elke statusrapportage deze scope-labels gebruiken:\n\n1. **HOST TOTAL** — `docker ps` count, geen grep-filter\n2. **MEDIA STACK** — exacte namen van relevante containers (radarr, sonarr, prowlarr, qbittorrent, flaresolverr, plex, sabnzbd)\n3. **VERIFIED** — alleen bewezen met output\n4. **NOT YET VERIFIED** — alles wat nog niet bewezen is\n\nGeen conclusies zonder bewijs. Geen \"niks mist\" zonder volledige audit.\n\n## qBittorrent Queueing & Speed\n\n**Kritieke settings die downloads blokkeren:**\n\n| Setting | Default | Impact |\n|---|---|---|\n| `max_active_downloads` | 3 | Maar N downloads tegelijk |\n| `max_active_torrents` | 5 | Maar N actieve torrents |\n| `queueing_enabled` | True | Rest staat \"Queued\" |\n| `dont_count_slow_torrents` | False | **Kritiek**: stalled bezetten wél slots als False! |\n| `slow_torrent_dl_rate_threshold` | 2 | KiB/s ondergrens voor \"slow\" (niet 1024!) |\n| `slow_torrent_ul_rate_threshold` | 2 | KiB/s ondergrens upload |\n| `slow_torrent_inactive_timer` | 600 | Seconden inactivity voor \"slow\" |\n| `up_limit` | 0 | Upload limit in B/s, te laag = download throttling |\n| `dl_limit` | 0 | Download limit, 0 = unlimited |\n| `scheduler_enabled` | False | ⚠️ Actief → kan snelheidslimieten forceren via schedule |\n\n**Belangrijk**: De API key names zijn anders dan de UI labels. Gebruik `dont_count_slow_torrents` — dit is de meest impactvolle setting: zonder deze (False) blijven stalled downloads met 0 seeds/peers actieve queue-slots bezetten, waardoor de hele queue vastloopt. Andere correcte keys: `slow_torrent_dl_rate_threshold` (niet `slow_torrent_dl_threshold`), `max_active_downloads`, `max_active_torrents`. Verifieer altijd met `GET /api/v2/app/preferences` voordat je `setPreferences` aanroept — foute keys worden stilletjes genegeerd (geen error, gewoon geen effect).\n\n**Pitfall 403 Forbidden**: qBittorrent cookies verlopen snel. Bij `setPreferences` met een verlopen cookie krijg je stom `403 Forbidden` zonder duidelijke error. Altijd opnieuw `/api/v2/auth/login` doen vóór schrijfacties. Leesacties met verlopen cookie geven lege JSON (`JSONDecodeError`), dus herlogin-check is: als GET leeg terugkomt → cookie verlopen.\n\nSymptomen van te lage limieten:\n- Torrents staan `queuedDL` ondanks beschikbare seeds\n- Down Speed 0 B/s, ETA ∞\n- Size 0 B (nog niet gestart met downloaden)\n- Meerdere torrents `Stalled`\n\n**Aanbevolen settings voor gezonde queue:**\n```\nmax_active_downloads: 3\nmax_active_torrents: 10\nqueueing_enabled: true\ndont_count_slow_torrents: true\nslow_torrent_dl_rate_threshold: 2\nslow_torrent_ul_rate_threshold: 2\nslow_torrent_inactive_timer: 600\n```\n\n## Diagnostic Commands\n\nMissing episodes checken:\n```bash\ncurl \"http://localhost:8989/api/v3/wanted/missing?apikey=KEY&pageSize=200\"\n```\n\nqBittorrent actieve downloads:\n```bash\ncurl -b /tmp/qbt_cookie.txt \"http://localhost:8080/api/v2/torrents/info\"\n```\n\nSonarr queue:\n```bash\ncurl \"http://localhost:8989/api/v3/queue?apikey=KEY\"\n```\n\n## Approval Avoidance\n\nGebruik `curl -o /tmp/file.json` + `python3 script.py` in plaats van `curl | python3 -c`.\nSchrijf scripts met `write_file`, voer uit met `terminal python3 script.py`.\nGeen pipes van curl naar interpreter — dit triggert security scans.\n\nZie ook skill `hermes-output-discipline` voor algemene output/approval regels.\n\n## CRITICAL: qBittorrent Seeding & Ratio Safety Rules\n\n**NOOIT `max_ratio=0` met `max_ratio_action=2` (remove) instellen!**\n\nDit verwijdert ALLE voltooide downloads inclusief bestanden die nog niet door Sonarr/Radarr\ngeïmporteerd zijn. Catastrofaal dataverlies (2026-07-07: 29 records beschadigd).\n\n### Veilige defaults\n\n```\nmax_ratio_enabled: false    # NIET aanzetten tenzij expliciet gewenst\nmax_ratio_action: 1         # 1=pause, NOOIT 2=remove\nmax_seeding_time_enabled: false\n```\n\n### Per-download-client in Sonarr/Radarr\n\n- `removeCompletedDownloads: false` — NOOIT true zetten; torrents worden dan verwijderd na import\n- `removeFailedDownloads: false` — zelfde reden\n- `Completed Download Handling: ON` — wél veilig, dit is import, niet delete\n- `autoRedownloadFailed: ON` — veilig\n\n### Damage Assessment\n\nNa een ratio-incident, check hasFile records met missing files. Zie `references/qbittorrent-safety-rules.md`.\n\n## Prowlarr Search Bridge Probleem\n\n**Bevinding 2026-07-07**: Sonarr's search via Prowlarr retourneert 0 resultaten voor\nTV series, terwijl Prowlarr direct search wél resultaten geeft (20+ voor WWII E02).\n\nOorzaken:\n- 1337x geblokkeerd door CloudFlare (FlareSolverr faalt met \"IP banned\")\n- TPB te traag/rate-limited voor TV queries\n- YTS is movies-only, synced niet naar Sonarr\n\n**Bewezen werkende indexers voor TV (2026-07-08):**\n- **LimeTorrents**: wel ge-synced naar Sonarr, vindt 80 Days episodes. Base URL: `https://www.limetorrents.fun`\n- **TPB**: vindt WWII, Tulsa King (via Prowlarr direct search, Sonarr's eigen search via TPB is traag)\n- **EZTV**: beschikbaar in Prowlarr maar sync-categorieën moeten handmatig gecheckt worden\n\nWorkaround: gebruik Prowlarr direct search (`/api/v1/search`) en grab via guid/magnet\nrechtstreeks naar qBittorrent. Zie `arr-stack-workflow` skill voor het fast-grab patroon.\n\nVoor volledige diagnostische stappen (Prowlarr vs Sonarr search vergelijking,\nFlareSolverr test, LimeTorrents fallback): zie `references/search-bridge-diagnostic.md`.\n\n## Usenet / SABnzbd Extension\n\nFor Flemish/Dutch content unavailable on public trackers, Usenet via SABnzbd is the alternative.\nSee `arr-stack-workflow/references/usenet-setup.md` for detailed setup guide and NZBIndex findings.\n", "path": "devops/media-stack-management/SKILL.md", "skill_dir": "/home/sjoe/.hermes/skills/devops/media-stack-management", "org_provenance": null, "linked_files": {"references": ["references/qbittorrent-queue-prefs.md", "references/search-bridge-diagnostic.md", "references/l4-snapshot-inspector.md", "references/sabnzbd-setup.md", "references/qbittorrent-safety-rules.md"]}, "usage_hint": "To view linked files, call skill_view(name, file_path) where file_path is e.g. 'references/api.md' or 'assets/config.yaml'", "required_environment_variables": [], "required_commands": [], "missing_required_environment_variables": [], "missing_credential_files": [], "missing_required_commands": [], "setup_needed": false, "setup_skipped": false, "readiness_status": "available", "_source_path": "/home/sjoe/.hermes/skills/devops/media-stack-management/SKILL.md"}
